&lt;project_context>
# 项目: faith-evidence-ie

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: rtx5090-4x
- SSH 账户: root（调用 ssh_execute / submit_training_job 时传入 account="root"）
- SSH 连接命令: ssh rtx5090-4x  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/autodl-tmp/freige

## ⚠️ 活跃约束（必须遵守）

- **C001**: 提交 eval 前必须 nvidia-smi 确认目标 GPU 显存占用 < 2GB，否则不提交、换卡或等待（原因：本 session 已 5 次因 eval 提交到被占用 GPU 导致 OOM，修复 eval 也因此作废。eval 推理需要接近全部显存，与训练共享必然 OOM。） [来自 D064]
- **C002**: 提交训练/评估任务时必须在命令前设置 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=X，禁止依赖 device_map="auto" 自动分配 GPU（原因：plan_011 和 rsft_ced_s45_train_r3 两次因未设 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 导致 device_map="auto" 跨 GPU 分布，造成 OOM 和资源冲突。已知 bug 重复发生。）
- **C003**: 提交训练任务时必须在命令前设置 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=X，禁止依赖 device_map="auto" 自动分配 GPU（原因：plan_011 和 rsft_ced_s45_train_r3 均因未设 CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES 导致模型跨 GPU 分布，造成 OOM 或显存冲突。已重复发生两次。）
- **C004**: Registry 标记 killed 时必须同时 kill -9 PID 并 ps 验证进程已终止，否则 killed 状态不可信（原因：rsft_ced_s45_train_r2 (PID 276148) registry 标记 killed 但进程未实际终止，占用 cuda:2 31.6GB 导致 r3 OOM。plan_011 也有类似模式。Reviewer WARNING。）


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
## 任务：在 cuda:1 上启动 plan_006 (Qwen3-1.7B Model Size Ablation) — 第一步 SFT

### 目标
在 Qwen3-1.7B 上复现 RSFT+CED pipeline 的第一步：SFT baseline 训练。验证方法对小模型的泛化性。

### 严格要求
**C001 合规**：提交前必须 `nvidia-smi` 确认 cuda:1 显存占用 < 2GB。如果不满足，**立即停止**并汇报，不要提交。

### 服务器环境
- 服务器：rtx5090-4x (SSH)
- 账号：root
- 项目路径：`/root/autodl-tmp/`
- Python 环境：`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`
- GPU：cuda:1（物理 GPU 1，CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1）
- 所有命令 cwd：`cd /root/autodl-tmp`

### 执行步骤

**Step 1: 环境检查**
```bash
nvidia-smi --query-gpu=index,memory.used,memory.total --format=csv,noheader
```
确认 GPU 1 显存 < 2GB。如果不满足，停止并汇报。

**Step 2: 检查 Qwen3-1.7B 模型**
```bash
ls -la /root/autodl-tmp/models/ | grep -i qwen
```
如果没有 Qwen3-1.7B（可能叫 Qwen3-1.7B 或 Qwen2.5-1.5B-Instruct 等），需要下载。
- 优先用 ModelScope（AutoDL 环境下速度快）：
  ```bash
  source /etc/network_turbo
  pip install modelscope -q
  python -c "from modelscope import snapshot_download; snapshot_download('Qwen/Qwen3-1.7B', cache_dir='/root/autodl-tmp/models')"
  ```
- 或者用 HF（设 HF_HOME=/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache）

**重要**：先 WebSearch 确认 "Qwen3-1.7B" 是否存在以及正确的 model ID。Qwen3 系列可能是 0.6B / 1.7B / 4B / 8B / 14B / 30B / 32B。确认后下载。

**Step 3: 参考现有 SFT 配置**
读取现有 Qwen3-4B SFT 的训练脚本和配置：
```bash
# 查找 SFT 训练脚本
ls /root/autodl-tmp/freige/train/
cat /root/autodl-tmp/freige/train/sft.py  # 或类似文件名
```

同时读取之前 SFT 训练实验的 registry 信息。之前 mvp2_sft_warmup 的 spec 包含了训练命令。关键参数：
- LoRA: rank=128, alpha=128, target_modules=all-linear
- 数据：/root/autodl-tmp/data/docred/train_annotated.json（3027 samples）
- Epochs: 3
- Batch size: 需要根据 1.7B 模型调整（显存更小，可以用更大 batch）

**Step 4: 准备 SFT 训练命令**
基于 4B 的配置，为 1.7B 调整：
- model_path 改为 Qwen3-1.7B 路径
- output_dir 改为 `/root/autodl-tmp/sft_output_1.7b`
- LoRA rank/alpha 可能需要缩小（1.7B 参数少，rank=128 可能占比过高）→ 参考 4B 的 trainable_params 比例(3.18%)来调整
- batch_size 可以稍大（1.7B 显存占用少）

**Step 5: 提交训练**
用 `submit_training_job` 提交，必须：
- exp_id: `plan_006_sft_1.7b`
- server: rtx5090-4x
- gpu_ids: ["cuda:1"]
- plan_id: plan_006
- 命令必须用 `bash -c "..."` 包裹

提交前在 registry 创建实验条目，hypothesis 写清楚：
"Qwen3-1.7B SFT baseline training for model size ablation (plan_006). 复现 4B SFT pipeline on smaller model, 验证 FREIGE 方法对模型规模的泛化性。Claim 1 robustness check (RC4)."

**Step 6: 验证启动**
提交后检查：
- `ps aux | grep python` 确认进程在跑
- `nvidia-smi` 确认 cuda:1 有显存占用
- tail 日志确认训练正常开始

### 产出
- 训练成功启动
- Registry 条目已创建并转 RUNNING
- 汇报：模型大小、LoRA 配置、预计训练时长

\"random_ign_f1\": 0.439677}, \"notes\": \"CED reranker (N=8, no training) rel_f1=0.4864, 超过 SFT greedy (+8.07pp) 和 RSFT 3-seed mean (+2.68pp)。Oracle 上界 rel_f1=0.6237 (+21.8pp vs SFT)，CED reranker 实现了 oracle ceiling 的 37% (0.0807/0.218)。CED-F1 Spearman r=0.3235 (p≈0)，相关性显著但中等——说明 CED 不是完美的 proxy 但有信号。Random baseline rel_f1=0.4538 说明 sampling diversity 本身就有 +4.8pp 收益，CED 在此基础上再加 +3.3pp。EDCR 下降 (0.6156 vs SFT 0.7118)，N=8 sampling 引入更多 distractor citations。推理耗时 5.8h (N=8 生成主导)。结论：CED 作为 inference-time reranker 有明确论文价值——零训练成本超越 RSFT，且 oracle ceiling 表明更好的 reward model 还有大幅空间。\"}, Based on the search results, here's comprehensive information about the Qwen3-1.7B model and its availability on ModelScope:

```text
## Qwen3-1.7B Model Overview

Qwen3 includes dense models available in sizes including 1.7B. Qwen3-1.7B is one of six dense models that are open-weighted under the Apache 2.0 license.

## Performance and Capabilities

Due to advancements in model architecture and training methods, Qwen3-1.7B performs as well as Qwen2.5-3B. The 1.7B model saw high download volumes from the embedded and mobile development community.

## Availability on ModelScope

The Qwen3-1.7B model can be downloaded via ModelScope. You can find all Qwen3 models by visiting ModelScope and searching for checkpoints with names starting with Qwen3-. Additionally, every Qwen3 model is downloadable from Hugging Face, GitHub, and ModelScope.

## Release Timeline

The Qwen3 model family was released on April 28, 2025, with all models licensed under the Apache 2.0 license. Since the current date is May 16, 2026, the model has been available for over a year.

## Context Window and Specifications

Qwen3-1.7B is characterized as an extra-small dense model with a 32K context window.
```

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
Now I have all the default parameters:
- per_device_batch_size=1
- gradient_accumulation_steps=16
- num_epochs=3
- learning_rate=2e-4
- warmup_ratio=0.05
- bf16=True
- lora_rank=64, lora_alpha=128
- QLoRA 4-bit

For the 4B SFT: 3027 samples / (1 * 16) = 189.2 → 190 steps per epoch. 3 epochs = 570 steps.


Looking at the existing checkpoints at steps 96 and 144, I'm trying to figure out what batch configuration would produce 48 steps per epoch. If that were the case, epoch 2 would end at step 96 and epoch 3 at step 144, which matches what I'm seeing—though the first checkpoint at step 48 appears to have been deleted.

Working through the math: with 3027 training samples, a 48-step epoch means an effective batch size of about 63. That could be per_device_batch_size=8 with gradient_accumulation_steps=8, or per_device_batch_size=4 with gradient_accumulation_steps=16. But the script defaults to per_device_batch_size=1 with gradient_accumulation_steps=16, which would give 190 steps per epoch instead—producing checkpoints at 190, 380, and so on. So the actual training must have used different batch settings than the defaults.

Now I need to focus on setting up the 1.7B model: downloading Qwen3-1.7B and configuring the training parameters. Since the 1.7B model is smaller, it'll fit more easily in memory on the RTX 5090—the 4-bit quantized weights should be around 1.1GB—so I can afford a larger per_device_batch_size like 4 or 8 while keeping gradient_accumulation_steps at 16. I should match the LoRA settings from the 4B training (lora_rank=64, lora_alpha=128) and think through what the appropriate LoRA ratio should be for this smaller model. With rank=64, the 4B SFT had the default trainable percentage. For the 1.7B model, I'll use the same LoRA configuration (rank=64, alpha=128) even though the trainable ratio will be higher due to fewer base parameters—that should work fine for this ablation. Now I need to set up the training command with the model path, output directory, batch size (trying 2 or 4 since 1.7B is smaller), gradient accumulation to maintain effective batch size, 3 epochs, and wandb tracking. But first I should download the model using ModelScope instead of HuggingFace, since the AutoDL proxy is too slow for large files.
```
